     .               /// Whether [`Self::is_valid_public_point`] decides subgroup membership

-- line 646 ----------------------------------------
-- line 697 ----------------------------------------
     .               /// J. Ong, "Point compression for Koblitz elliptic curves", IACR
     .               /// ePrint 2009/086 (two-level halving for `#E = 4ℓ`).  The closed
     .               /// form here only inlines the half's trace,
     .               /// `Tr(u) = Tr(u²) = Tr(y) + Tr(x·H(x))`, so no square root or second
     .               /// half-trace is needed.
     .               ///
     .               /// Not constant-time (table lookups indexed by the coordinates,
     .               /// early exit on `Tr(x)`): intended for public points only.

     .           /// **Point Addition** on a binary curve (Putranto et al. §3, formula
     .           /// reproduced in the module header).
     .           ///
     .           /// Cases:
     .           /// - One operand is `O` → return the other.
     .           /// - `P₁ = P₂` → defer to [`point_double`].
     .           /// - `P₁ = −P₂` → return `O`.
     .           /// - Otherwise: λ = (y₁+y₂)/(x₁+x₂), x₃ = λ² + λ + x₁ + x₂ + a,
     .           ///   y₃ = λ(x₁+x₃) + x₃ + y₁.

        .               /// **Fermat-little-theorem inversion** via Itoh-Tsujii.
        .               ///
        .               /// `a^(2^m − 2) = a^(-1)` in `F_{2^m}` (since `|F_{2^m}^*| =
        .               /// 2^m − 1`).  Itoh-Tsujii decomposes `2^m − 2 = 2 · (2^(m−1)
        .               /// − 1)` and exploits the binary structure of `m − 1` so that
        .               /// only `O(log m)` multiplications and `O(m)` squarings are
        .               /// needed (rather than `m − 1` multiplications via naïve
        .               /// square-and-multiply).
        .               ///
        .               /// Concretely:
        .               ///   1. Compute `b_k = a^{2^{2^k} − 1}` for increasing `k`,
        .               ///      using `b_{k+1} = b_k · (b_k)^{2^{2^k}}`.
        .               ///   2. Combine `b_k`'s per the binary expansion of `m − 1`.
        .               ///   3. Final squaring: result = `(combined)²`.
      640 ( 0.00%)      pub fn flt_inverse(&self, irreducible: &IrreduciblePoly) -> Option<Self> {
      384 ( 0.00%)          super::operation_tally::tick(self.m, "inverse_informational");
        .                   if self.is_zero() {
        .                       return None;
        .                   }
        .                   let m = self.m;
        .                   // We want a^(2^m − 2) = a^(2 · (2^(m−1) − 1)).
        .                   // Let n = m − 1.
        .                   let n = m - 1;
        .                   // Compute a^(2^n − 1) using Itoh-Tsujii (binary-of-n decomposition).
        .                   let mut bits: Vec<u32> = Vec::new();
        .                   let mut nn = n;
    2,752 ( 0.00%)          while nn > 0 {

      512 ( 0.00%)              nn >>= 1;
